## n8n 中的语义化版本

n8n 使用[语义化版本](https://semver.org/)。所有版本号都采用 `MAJOR.MINOR.PATCH`（主版本.次版本.补丁版本）格式。版本号的递增规则如下：

* MAJOR（主版本）：进行不兼容的变更，可能需要用户采取行动。
* MINOR（次版本）：以向后兼容的方式添加功能。
* PATCH（补丁版本）：进行向后兼容的 bug 修复。

## n8n@0.234.0

本版本包含新功能、新节点、节点增强和错误修复。

{% hint style="warning" %}
**不稳定版本**

此版本（截至 2023 年 7 月 4 日）被视为不稳定版本。n8n 建议直接升级到 0.234.1。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="warning" %}
**不可逆的数据库迁移**

此版本包含一次数据库迁移，把凭证和工作流 ID 改为使用 nanoId 字符串。在某些环境中，这次迁移可能需要一段时间才能完成。此变更不会破坏任何使用旧数字 ID 的东西。

如果你升级到 0.234.0，将无法回滚到更早的版本。
{% endhint %}

完整发布详情请参阅 GitHub 上的 [Releases](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/releases)。

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Debug Helper

Debug Helper（调试助手）节点可用于触发不同类型的错误或生成随机数据集，帮助测试 n8n 工作流。

[Debug Helper node documentation](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.debughelper).
{% endhint %}

### 新功能

值得注意的新功能。

#### Resource mapper UI component

本版本引入一个新的 UI 组件：资源映射器（resource mapper）。该组件对节点创建者很有用。如果你的节点执行 insert、update 或 upsert 操作，你需要以所集成服务支持的格式从节点发送数据。通常需要在发送数据的节点前使用一个 Set（设置）节点，使数据匹配所连接服务的模式（schema）。资源映射器 UI 组件提供了一种直接在节点内把数据整理成所需格式的方式。

节点构建者的指南请参阅 [节点用户界面元素 | 资源映射器](https://app.gitbook.com/s/r7wKI4I1BgdBCuq5Cvcx/create-nodes/build-your-node/reference/node-ui-elements#resource-mapper)。

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Execution Data

为工作流执行保存元数据。然后你可以在 **Executions（执行）** 列表中按这些数据搜索。

[Execution Data node documentation](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.executiondata).
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### LDAP node

LDAP 节点允许你在 n8n 工作流中与 LDAP 服务器交互。

[LDAP node documentation](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.ldap).
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### LoneScale node

把 n8n 与 [LoneScale](https://www.lonescale.com/) 集成，这是一个购买意向数据平台。

[LoneScale node documentation](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.lonescale).
{% endhint %}

## n8n@0.227.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.226.2...n8n@0.227.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-05-03

本版本包含新功能、节点增强和错误修复。

完整发布详情请参阅 GitHub 上的 [Releases](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/releases)。

### 节点增强

* 全面改造 the Microsoft Excel 365 node, improve the UI making it easier to configure, improve error handling, and fix issues

### 弃用功能

本版本弃用以下功能：

* `EXECUTIONS_PROCESS` environment variable
* Running n8n in own mode. Main mode is now the default. Use [Queue mode](https://app.gitbook.com/s/jm0ZYRpZIPWge2ZSiDYO/host-n8n/configure-n8n/scaling/enable-queue-mode) if you need full execution isolation.
* `WEBHOOK_TUNNEL_URL` flag. Replaced by `WEBHOOK_URL`
* 支持for MySQL and MariaDB as n8n backend databases. n8n will remove support completely in version 1.0. n8n recommends using PostgreSQL instead

**发布日期：** 2023-04-26

本版本包含新功能、节点增强和错误修复。

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

请注意，此版本包含对 `extractDomain` 和 `isDomain` 的破坏性变更。你可以[在此](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#02260)阅读更多相关信息。
{% endhint %}

完整发布详情请参阅 GitHub 上的 [Releases](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/releases)。

### 新功能

*   A new command to get information about licenses for self-hosted users:

    ```sh
    n8n license:info
    ```

### 节点增强

* Nodes that use SQL, such as the PostgresSQL node, now have a better SQL editor for writing custom queries.
* 全面改造 the Google BigQuery node to support executing queries, improve the UI making it easier to configure, improve error handling, and fix issues

## n8n@0.225.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.224.1...n8n@0.225.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-04-19

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
本版本引入[变量（Variables）](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/code-in-n8n/define-custom-variables)。你现在可以创建变量，在 n8n 工作流中存储和复用值。这是 n8n 支持[环境（Environments）](https://app.gitbook.com/s/wMJrGrimpx3PxCJpUswm/use-source-control-and-environments)这一更大项目的第一阶段。
{% endhint %}

* Core：新增HTTP Request 节点中的 Google 服务账号认证支持。
* GitLab 节点：新增文件列表操作的 **Additional Parameters（附加参数）**。
* MySQL 节点：该节点已全面改造

### 错误修复

* Core：修复公共 API 中损坏的 API 权限。
* Core：修复pairedItem 返回错误数据的问题。
* Core：改进SAML 连接测试结果视图。
* Core：让在所有节点类型上提供 getExecutionId。
* Core：跳过为有名和姓的用户跳过 SAML 引导。
* 编辑器：新增为 prepend input 添加内边距。
* 编辑器：清理demo/video experiment
* Editor: Enterprise features missing with user management.

## n8n@0.224.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.223.0...n8n@0.224.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-04-12

本版本包含新节点、更新和错误修复。

### 新节点

本版本引入 [TOTP](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.totp) 节点。这是一个新的核心节点，让你可以在工作流中生成 TOTP（基于时间的一次性密码）。

### 错误修复

* Code 节点：更新vm2（解决 CVE-2023-29017 安全漏洞）。
* Core：应用在使用自定义 REST 端点时不应崩溃
* Core：不要 execute workflowExecuteBefore hook when resuming executions from a waiting state.
* Core：修复问题 where sub workflows would display as running forever after failure to start.
* Core：更新xml2js（升级以解决 CVE-2023-0842 安全漏洞）。
* 编辑器：移除mergeDeep in favor of lodash merge
* HTTP Request 节点：恢复详细的错误消息。

### 贡献者

[Loganaden Velvindron](https://github.com/loganaden)

## n8n@0.223.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.222.1...n8n@0.223.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-04-05

本版本包含新功能和错误修复。

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

请注意，此版本包含一个破坏性变更。最低 Node.js 版本现在是 v16。你可以[在此](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#02230)阅读更多相关信息。
{% endhint %}

### 新功能

* Core：转换把 `eventBus` 控制器改为装饰器风格并改进权限。
* Core：防止启用 SAML 时非 owner 的密码重置（为即将推出的功能做准备）。
* Core：从环境读取临时许可证并清理 `ee` 标志
* 编辑器：允许用 Tab 键接受补全。
* 编辑器：启用节点详情视图打开时保存工作流。
* 编辑器：SSO 引导（为即将推出的功能做准备）
* 编辑器：SSO 设置（为即将推出的功能做准备）

### 节点增强

* Filter 节点：显示被丢弃的条目。
* HTTP Request 节点：默认跟随重定向
* Postgres 节点：全面改造节点。
* ServiceNow 节点：新增更新事件（incident）时对工作备注（work notes）的支持。
* SSH 节点：隐藏SSH 凭证中的私钥。

### 错误修复

* 映射时为布尔值添加可拖放状态
* Compare Datasets 节点：模糊比较未比较其中一个输入中缺失的键
* Compare Datasets 节点：修复跳过字段中的点号表示法支持。
* Core：导入期间停用活跃工作流
* Core：停止在 `jsonStringify` 中把重复项标记为循环引用的问题。
* Core：停止使用 `util.types.isProxy` 跟踪增强对象的问题。
* Core：修复无数据时的 curl 导入错误。
* Core：处理在 `jsonStringify` 中正确处理 Date 和 RegExp。
* Core：处理在 `augmentObject` 中正确处理 Date 和 RegExp 对象。
* Core：防止`augmentObject` 创建无限深层代理的问题。
* Core：服务账号私钥作为密码字段
* Core：更新锁定文件。
* Core：等待中的工作流不停止的问题
* Date & Time 节点：新增日期与时间顶部解释表达式的信息框。
* Date & Time 节点：转换把 Luxon DateTime 对象转换为 ISO。
* 编辑器：新增把 `$if`、`$min`、`$max` 添加到根表达式自动补全。
* 编辑器：限制过度热心的条目访问检查
* 编辑器：禁用在表达式编辑器中禁用 Grammarly。
* 编辑器：禁用无用户管理的桌面端密码重置。
* 编辑器：修复连接丢失悬停文本不显示的问题。
* 编辑器：修复问题 preventing execution preview loading when in an Iframe.
* 编辑器：修复带特殊字符的映射。
* 编辑器：防止复制未保存工作流时出现错误的问题。
* 编辑器：防止NDV 模式视图分页。
* 编辑器：支持带修饰键的退格删除。
* Google Sheets 节点：修复insertOrUpdate 单元格使用对象更新。
* HTML Extract 节点：支持JSON 属性中的点号表示法。
* HTTP Request 节点：修复 AWS credentials to stop removing URL parameters for STS.
* HTTP Request 节点：在 never fail 选项下正确刷新令牌
* HTTP Request 节点：支持JSON 请求体中的点号表示法。
* LinkedIn 节点：更新API 版本。
* Redis 节点：修复问题 hash set not working as expected.

## n8n@0.222.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.221.2...n8n@0.222.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-03-30

本版本包含新功能，包括执行列表的自定义过滤器，以及一个用于在工作流中过滤条目的新节点。

{% hint style="info" %}
**升级到 0.222.1**

请直接升级到 0.222.1。
{% endhint %}

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
本版本对执行列表进行了改进。你现在可以保存[自定义执行数据](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/understand-workflows/understand-executions/customize-executions-data)，并用来过滤[全部执行](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/understand-workflows/understand-executions/view-all-executions)和[单个工作流执行](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/understand-workflows/understand-executions/view-executions-for-a-single-workflow)两个列表。
{% endhint %}

* 新增test overrides
* Core：改进 LDAP/SAML toggle and tests.
* Core：限制 user invites when SAML is enabled.
* Core：让 OAuth2 error handling consistent with success handling.
* 编辑器：修复 ResourceLocator dropdown style.

### 新节点

本版本引入 [Filter（过滤器）](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.filter)节点。该节点允许你根据条件过滤条目。如果条目满足条件，Filter 节点会把它传给 Filter 节点输出中的下一个节点；如果条目不满足条件，Filter 节点会把它从输出中省略。

### 错误修复

* Core: Assign `properties.success` earlier to set `executionStatus` correctly.
* Core：不要 mark duplicates as circular references in `jsonStringify`.
* Core：不要 use `util.types.isProxy` for tracking of augmented objects.
* Core：确保 that all non-lazy-loaded community nodes get post-processed correctly.
* Core：Force-upgrade decode-uri-component（升级以解决 CVE-2022-38900 安全漏洞）
* Core：Force-upgrade http-cache-semantics（升级以解决 CVE-2022-25881 安全漏洞）
* Core：处理 `Date` and `RegExp` correctly in `jsonStringify`.
* Core：处理 `Date` and `RegExp` objects in `augmentObject`.
* Core：改进节点中的 Axios 错误处理。
* Core：改进 community nodes loading.
* Core: Initialize queue in the webhook server as well.
* Core: Persist `CurrentAuthenticationMethod` setting change.
* Core：移除 circular references from Code and push message.
* Core：要求 authentication on icons and nodes/credentials types static files.
* Core：返回 SAML service provider URls with configuration.
* Core: Service account private key should display as a password field.
* Core：升级Luxon（升级以解决 CVE-2023-22467 安全漏洞）。
* Core：升级simple-git（升级以解决 CVE-2022-25912 安全漏洞）。
* Core：升级SQLite3（升级以解决 CVE-2022-43441 安全漏洞）。
* Core：升级Convict（升级以解决 CVE-2023-0163 安全漏洞）。
* Core：等待中的工作流不停止的问题
* 编辑器：修复连接丢失悬停文本不显示的问题。
* 编辑器：修复问题 preventing execution preview loading when in an iframe.
* 编辑器：使用 credentials when fetching node and credential types.
* Google Sheets 节点：修复 `insertOrUpdate` cell update with object.
* HTTP Request 节点：新增 streaming to binary response.
* HTTP Request 节点：修复AWS 凭证自动解析 URL。
* HTTP Request 节点：修复 AWS credentials to stop removing URL parameters for STS.
* Split In Batches Node: Roll back changes in v1 and create v2.
* 更新PostHog no-capture

### 贡献者

[Manish Dhanwal](https://github.com/ManishDhanwal07)

## n8n@0.217.2

## n8n@0.217.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.216.1...n8n@0.217.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-02-23

本版本包含新功能和错误修复，包括节点面板和执行列表的改进，同时弃用了 Read Binary File（读取二进制文件）节点。

### 新功能

* 新增new event hooks to support telemetry around the new onboarding experience
* 更新nodes to set required path type
* Core：新增 configurable execution history limit. Use this to improve performance when self-hosting. Refer to [Execution Data | Enable data pruning ](https://app.gitbook.com/s/jm0ZYRpZIPWge2ZSiDYO/host-n8n/configure-n8n/scaling/manage-execution-data#enable-executions-pruning)for more information.
* Core：新增 execution runData recovery and status field. This allows us to show execution statuses on the **Executions** list.
* Core：新增 SAML feature flag. This is preparatory for an upcoming feature.
* 编辑器：改进 ments to the nodes panel search. When searching in root view, n8n now displays results from both trigger and regular nodes. When searching in a category view, n8n shows results from the category, and also suggests results from other categories.
* 隐藏sensitive value in authentication header credentials and authentication query credentials
* 支持feature flag evaluation server side
* 弃用Read Binary File node. Use the [Read Binary Files](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.readwritefile) node instead

### 错误修复

* Baserow 节点：修复**Get All** not correctly using filters 相关的问题。
* Compare Datasets Node: UI tweaks and fixes.
* Core：不要 allow arbitrary path traversal in BinaryDataManager.
* Core：不要 allow arbitrary path traversal in the credential-translation endpoint.
* Core：不要 explicitly bypass authentication on URLs containing `.svg`.
Core：不要在 PurgeInvalidWorkflowConnections 迁移中移除空的输出连接数组。
* Core：修复 execution status filters.
* Core：使用 r update endpoint should only allow updating email, firstName, and lastName.
* Discord 节点：修复 wrong error message being displayed.
* Discourse 节点：修复credential test not working 相关的问题。
* Editor: apply correct IRunExecutionData to finished workflow.
* 编辑器：修复zoom and canvas nodes connections 相关的问题。
* 编辑器：修复 unexpected date rendering on front-end.
* 编辑器：移除 `crashed` status from filter.
* 修复typo in error messages when a property doesn't exist
* 修复saving an active workflow without triggers would cause n8n to be stuck
* Google Calendar 节点：修复 incorrect labels for start and end times when getting all events.
* Postgres 节点：修复 for tables containing field named JSON.
* AWS S3 节点：修复get many buckets not outputting data 相关的问题。

## n8n@0.215.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.214.3...n8n@0.215.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-02-10

本版本包含新功能、节点增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

* 重构n8n Desktop user management experience
* Core：新增对 WebSockets as an alternative to server-sent events. This introduces a new way for n8n's backend to push changes to the UI. The default is still server-sent events. If you're experiencing issues with the UI not updating, try changing to WebSockets by setting the `N8N_PUSH_BACKEND` environment variable to `websocket` 的支持。
* 编辑器：新增 autocomplete for objects.
* 编辑器：新增 autocomplete for expressions to the HTML editor component.

### 节点增强

* Edit Image 节点：新增对 WebP image format 的支持。
* HubSpot Trigger 节点：新增 conversation events.

### 错误修复

* Core：禁用 transactions on SQLite migrations that use PRAGMA foreign\_keys.
* Core：确保 expression extension doesn't fail with optional chaining.
* Core：修复 import command for workflows with old format (affects workflows created before user management was introduced).
* Core：停止 copying icons to cache.
编辑器：防止为没有输入槽位的节点创建输入连接。
* Error workflow now correctly checks for subworkflow permissions.
* ActiveCampaign 节点：修复 additional fields not being sent when updating account contacts.
* Linear 节点：修复Issue States not loading correctly 相关的问题。
* MySQL migration parses database contents if necessary (fix for MariaDB).

### 贡献者

[Kirill](https://github.com/chrtkv)

## n8n@0.214.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.213.0...n8n@0.214.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-02-03

本版本包含新功能、节点增强和错误修复。 The expressions editor now supports autocomplete for some built in data transformation functions. The new features also include two of interest to node builders: a way to allow users to drag and drop data keys, and the new HTML editor component.

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

请注意，此版本包含对 Luxon 的破坏性变更。你可以[在此](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#02140)阅读更多相关信息。
{% endhint %}

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Autocomplete in the Extension editor

数据转换函数现在在 Expression（表达式）编辑器中支持自动补全。
{% endhint %}

* Core: export OpenAPI spec for external tools.
* Core：设置 custom Cache-Control headers for static assets.
* Core：简化 pagination in declarative node design.
* 编辑器：支持 mapping keys with drag and drop. Any field with the hint **Enter the field name as text** should now support mapping a data key using drag and drop. Node builders can enable this in their own nodes. Refer to [Creating nodes | UI elements](https://app.gitbook.com/s/r7wKI4I1BgdBCuq5Cvcx/create-nodes/build-your-node/reference/node-ui-elements#support-drag-and-drop-for-data-keys) for more information.
* 编辑器：新增 [HTML editor component](https://app.gitbook.com/s/r7wKI4I1BgdBCuq5Cvcx/create-nodes/build-your-node/reference/node-ui-elements#html) for use in parameters. This means node builders can now use the HTML editor that n8n uses in the HTML node as a UI component.
* Editor: append expressions in fixed values when mapping to string and JSON inputs.
* 编辑器：继续 to show mapping tooltip after dismiss.
* Editor: roll out schema view.

### 节点增强

* FTP 节点：流式传输上传和下载的二进制数据流式传输。
* Notion 节点：新增对 image blocks 的支持。
* OpenAI 节点：新增 **Frequency Penalty** and **Presence Penalty** to the node options for the text resource.
* Salesforce 节点：新增 **Has Opted Out Of Email** field to lead resource options.
* SSH 节点：流式传输上传和下载的二进制数据流式传输。
* Write Binary File 节点：流式传输 binary data for writes.
* YouTube Node: switch upload operation over to streaming and resumable uploads API.

### 错误修复

* 新增paired item to the most used nodes
* Core：修复 OAuth2 client credentials not always working.
* Core：修复 populating of node custom API call options.
* Core：修复 value resolution in declarative node design.
* Core：防止 shared user details being saved alongside execution data.
* Core：回退 custom API option injecting.
* 编辑器：新增 SMTP info translation link slot.
* Editor: change executions title to match menu.
* 编辑器：修复 JSON field completions while typing.
* Editor: handling router errors when navigation is canceled by user.
* 编辑器：设置 max width for executions list.
* 编辑器：停止 unsaved changes popup display when navigating away from an untouched workflow.
* 编辑器：修复 workflow executions view.
* Invoice Ninja 节点：修复 line items not being correctly set for quotes and invoices.
* Linear 节点：修复分页问题 for get all issues.。
* Mailchimp 节点：修复 webhook recreation.
* 防止unnecessarily touching `updatedAt` when n8n starts
* Schedule Trigger Node: change scheduler behaviour for intervals days and hours.
* Set 节点：修复 behaviour when selecting `continueOnFail` and `pairedItem`.

## n8n@0.213.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.212.1...n8n@0.213.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-01-27

本版本引入 LDAP，以及一个用于在 n8n 中处理 HTML 的新节点，同时包含节点增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### LDAP

本版本在自托管 Enterprise 和 Cloud Enterprise 套餐上引入 LDAP 支持。有关此功能的更多信息，请参阅 [LDAP](https://app.gitbook.com/s/wMJrGrimpx3PxCJpUswm/manage-users-and-access/verify-user-identity/connect-ldap)。
{% endhint %}

* 简化Node Details View by moving authentication details to the Credentials modal
* 改进workflow list performance

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### HTML node

n8n 有一个新的 [HTML 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.html)。它取代了 HTML Extract 节点，并新增生成 HTML 模板的功能。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* GitLab 节点：新增 file resource and operations.
* JIRA Software 节点：引入 resource locator component to improve UX.
* Send Email node: this node has been overhauled.

### 错误修复

* Core：不要 crash express app on unhandled rejected promises.
* Core：处理 missing binary metadata in download URLs.
* Core: upsert (update and insert) credentials and workflows in the `import:` commands.
* Core: validate numeric IDs in the public API.
* 编辑器：不要 request workflow data twice when opening a workflow.
* Editor: execution list micro optimization.
* 编辑器：修复 node authentication options ordering and hiding options based on node version.
* 编辑器：修复 save modal appearing after duplicating a workflow.
* 编辑器：防止 workflow execution list infinite no network error.
* Extension being too eager and making calls when it shouldn't.
* Google Drive 节点：使用 correct MIME type on converted downloads.
* HelpScout 节点：修复 tag search not working when getting all conversations.
* Notion (Beta) 节点：修复 create database page with multiple relation IDs not working.
* 更新**Sign in with Google** button to properly match design guidelines

### 贡献者

* [Devin Buhl](https://github.com/onedr0p)
* [Sven Ziegler](https://github.com/svzi)

* Core: Throw error in UI on expression referencing missing node but don't fail execution.
* DB revert command shouldn't run full migrations before each revert.
* 编辑器：禁用 data pinning on multiple output node types.
* 编辑器：不要 overwrite `window.onerror` in production.
* Editor: Execution page bug fixes.
* 编辑器：修复 event bus test.
* 编辑器：隐藏 data pinning discoverability tooltip in execution view.
* Editor: Mapping tooltip dismiss.
* Editor: Recover from unsaved finished execution.
* 编辑器：设置 ting NDV session ID.
* First/last being extended on proxy objects.
* 处理memory issues gracefully
* PayPal Trigger Node: Omit verification in sandbox environment.
* 报告app startup and database migration errors to Sentry
* 运行every database migration inside a transaction
* Upgrade class-validator（升级以解决 CVE-2019-18413 安全漏洞）
* Zoom 节点：新增 notice about deprecation of Zoom JWT app support.

### Known issues

在表达式中使用[可选链操作符](https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Operators/Optional_chaining)时，你可能会遇到错误。如果发生这种情况，请暂时避免使用该操作符。

## n8n@0.210.2

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.210.1...n8n@0.210.2)。
**发布日期：** 2023-01-09

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Typeahead for expressions

使用[表达式](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/work-with-data/expressions-versus-data-nodes)时，n8n 现在会在你输入时提供建议。

<img src=".gitbook/assets/0.210.2-expressions-type-ahead.gif" alt="&#x22;Animated GIF showing typeahead in action&#x22;" data-size="original">
{% endhint %}

### 错误修复

* Core：修复 crash of manual workflow executions for unsaved workflows.
* Editor: omit pairedItem from proxy completions.
* 编辑器：防止 refresh on submit in credential edit modal.
* Google Sheets 节点：修复 for auto-range detection.
* Read Binary File 节点：不要 crash the execution when the source file doesn't exist.
* 移除anonymous ID from tracking calls
* 让以下不再发生：OOM crashes in Execution Data pruning
* 更新links for user management and SMTP help

## n8n@0.210.1

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.210.0...n8n@0.210.1)。
**发布日期：** 2023-01-05

这是一个错误修复（bug fix）版本。 It also contains a new feature to support user management without SMTP set up.

### 新功能

#### Invite link for users on self-hosted n8n

在更早版本的自托管 n8n 中，用户管理功能需要你的 n8n 实例配置 SMTP 才能工作。用户管理需要 SMTP 来发送邀请邮件。

0.210.1 引入邀请链接，你可以复制并手动发送给用户。n8n 仍然建议配置 SMTP，因为密码重置需要它。

### 错误修复

* Google Sheets 节点：修复 an issue that was causing append and update operations to fail for numeric values.
* 解决external hooks

## n8n@0.210.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.209.4...n8n@0.210.0)。
**发布日期：** 2023-01-05

本版本引入两个主要新功能：日志流（log streaming）和安全审计（security audits），同时包含节点增强、错误修复和性能改进。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Log streaming

本版本为 Enterprise 自托管套餐和自定义 Cloud 套餐的用户引入[日志流](https://app.gitbook.com/s/wMJrGrimpx3PxCJpUswm/observe-and-log/stream-logs-to-external-systems)。日志流允许你把 n8n 的事件发送到自己的日志工具，从而在你自己的告警和日志流程中管理 n8n 监控。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Security audit

本版本新增[安全审计](https://app.gitbook.com/s/jm0ZYRpZIPWge2ZSiDYO/host-n8n/configure-n8n/security/run-security-audits)功能。你现在可以对 n8n 实例运行安全审计，以检测常见安全问题。
{% endhint %}

* Core：新增对 Redis 6+ ACLs system using username in queue mode. Add the `QUEUE_BULL_REDIS_USERNAME` environment variable 的支持。

### 节点增强

* Compare Datasets 节点：新增 an option for fuzzy compare.

### 错误修复

* 应用credential overwrites recursively. This ensures that overwrites defined for a parent credential type also apply to all credentials extending it
* Core：启用 full manual execution of a workflow using the error trigger.
* Core：修复 OAuth credential creation using the API.
* Core：修复workflow lastUpdated field 相关的问题。
* Editor: clear node creator and scrim on workspace reset.
* 编辑器：修复 an infinite loop while loading executions that aren't on the current executions list.
* 编辑器：让 node title non-editable in executions view.
* 编辑器：防止 scrim on executable triggers.
* 编辑器：支持 tabbing away from inline expression editor.
* 修复executions bulk deletion
* Google Sheets 节点：修复 exception when no **Values to Send** are set.
* Respond to Webhook 节点：修复 issue that caused the content-type header to be overwritten.
* Slack 节点：新增 missing channels:read OAuth2 scope.

### Performance improvements

* 懒加载public API dependencies to reduce baseline memory usage
* 懒加载queue mode and analytics dependencies

### 贡献者

[Thomas S.](https://github.com/intel44)

* 编辑器：新增 usage and plans page to Desktop.

编辑器：在空的参数输入中按 **=** 会切换到表达式模式。

## n8n@0.207.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.206.1...n8n@0.207.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-12-12

本版本新增画布上的撤销/重做操作支持，并包含错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Undo/redo

你现在可以在画布上撤销和重做操作。

使用 `ctrl`/`cmd` + `z` 撤销，`ctrl`/`cmd` + `shift` + `z` 重做。

目前，n8n 支持对以下画布操作进行撤销/重做：

* Adding nodes
* Deleting nodes
* Adding connections
* Deleting connections
* Moving nodes
* Moving connections
* Import workflow (from file/from URL)
* Copy/paste nodes
* Renaming nodes
* Duplicating nodes
* Disabling/enabling nodes
{% endhint %}

* App integration actions are now displayed in the nodes pane.
* 新增sharing permissions info for workflow sharees
* 处理sharing features when the user skips instance owner setup
* 更新credential test error message for credential sharees

### 错误修复

* Core：移除 nodeGetter.
* Core: Increase workflow reactivation max timeout to one day.
* Core：解决一个问题 listing executions with Postgres.
* Core：移除 foreign credentials when copying nodes or duplicating workflow.
* Core：升级 sse-channel to mitigate CVE-2019-10744.
* Core：使用 license-sdk v1.6.1.
* Editor: avoid adding Manual Trigger node when webhook node is added.
* 编辑器：修复 credential sharing issues handler when no matching ID or name.
* 编辑器：修复 for broken tab navigation.
* Editor: schema view shows checkbox in case of empty data.
* 编辑器：停止 returning UNKNOWN ERROR in the response if an actual error message is available.
* 编辑器：更新 duplicate workflow action.
* Move Binary Data Node: stringify objects before encoding them in MoveBinaryData.
* Split In Batches 节点：修复pairedItem 相关的问题。

## n8n@0.205.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.204.0...n8n@0.205.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-12-02

本版本包含对表达式编辑器的全面改造、节点增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Expressions editor usability overhaul

本版本包含表达式编辑器的易用性增强。编辑器现在包含颜色信号来指示语法是否有效，以及更好的错误消息和提示。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* Facebook Graph APInode: update to support API version 15.
* Google Calendar 节点：引入 resource locator component to help users retrieve calendar parameters.
* Postmark Trigger 节点：更新 credentials so they can be used with the HTTP Request node (for custom API calls).
* Todoist 节点：更新 to use API version 2.

### 错误修复

* Core：确保 executions list is properly filtered for all users.
* Core：修复 `$items().length` in Execute Once mode.
* Core: mark binary data to be deleted when pruning executions.
* Core: OAuth2 scope saved to database fix.
* 编辑器：修复 slots rendering of NodeCreator's NoResults component.
* Editor: JSON view values can be mapped like keys.
* AWS SNS 节点：修复 a pagination issue.
* Google Sheets 节点：修复 exception if no matching rows are found.
* Google Sheets 节点：修复 for append operation if no empty rows in sheet.
* Microsoft Outlook 节点：修复 binary attachment upload.
* Pipedrive 节点：解决 properties not working.
* Lazy load nodes for credentials testing.
* Credential overwrites should take precedence over credential default values.
* 移除background for resource ownership selector
* 更新padding for resource filters dropdown
* 更新size of select components in filters dropdown
* 更新workflow save button type and design and share button type

**发布日期：** 2022-11-24

本版本包含性能增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

* Core: lazy-load nodes and credentials to reduce baseline memory usage.
* Core：使用 longer stack traces when error reporting is enabled.

### 错误修复

* Core：修复 $items().length behavior in executeOnce mode.

* Core：使用 CredentialsOverwrites when testing credentials.

* 编辑器：防止 node-creator tabs from showing when toggled by CanvasAddButton.
* Editor: table view column limit tooltip.
* 编辑器：修复 broken n8n-info-tip slots.
* IF 节点：修复 "Is Empty" and "Is Not Empty" operation failures for date objects.
* 移除redundant `await` in nodes API request functions without try/catch
* Schedule 节点：修复 inconsistent behavior with cron and weekly intervals.
* Workflow activation shouldn't crash if one of the credential is invalid.

## n8n@0.202.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.201.0...n8n@0.202.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-11-10

本版本包含核心产品改进和错误修复。

### 新功能

* API: report unhandled app crashes using Sentry.
* API：设置 up error tracking using Sentry.
* Core：新增 ownership, sharing and credential details to `GET /workflows` in n8n's internal API.
* Editor: when building nodes, you can now add a property with type `notice` to your credentials `properties`.This was previously available in nodes but not credentials. Refer to [Node UI elements](https://app.gitbook.com/s/r7wKI4I1BgdBCuq5Cvcx/create-nodes/build-your-node/reference/node-ui-elements) for more information.

### 错误修复

* API：不要 use names for type ORM connections.
* Core：修复 manual execution of pinned trigger on main mode.
* Core：流式传输 line multiple pinned triggers behavior.
* Editor: Curb argument linting for `$input.first()` and `$input.last()`
* 编辑器：修复 duplicate bug when new workflow is open.
* 编辑器：修复 for incorrect execution saving indicator in executions view.
* 编辑器：修复 for OAuth authorization.
* 编辑器：修复 workflow activation from the Workflows view.
* 编辑器：修复 workflow back button navigation.
* 编辑器：防止 adding of the start node when importing workflow in the demo mode.
* 编辑器：显示 string numbers and null properly in JSON view.
* Editor: Switch CodeNodeEditor linter parser to esprima-next.
* Editor: Tweak dragged mapping state.
* 编辑器：更新 workflow buttons spacings.
* 编辑器：使用 base path in workflow preview component URL.
* HTTP Request 节点：显示 error cause in the output.
* HTTP Request 节点：使用 data in **Put Output in Field** field.
* HubSpot 节点：新增 notice to HubSpot credentials about API Key Sunset.
* Notion Trigger (Beta) 节点：修复 Notion trigger polling strategy.
* Raindrop 节点：更新 access token URL.
* SendInBlue 节点：修复credential name 中的拼写错误。
* 更新E2E testing ENV variables

### 贡献者

[feelgood-interface](https://github.com/feelgood-interface)\
[Ugo Bataillard](https://github.com/knshiro)

## n8n@0.201.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.200.1...n8n@0.201.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-11-02

本版本包含工作流和节点增强，以及错误修复。

### 新功能

* Core: reimplement blocking workflow updates on interim changes.
* Editor: block the UI in node details view when the workflow is listening for an event.
* Performance improvements

### 节点增强

Venafi TLS Protect Cloud 节点：让签发模板（issuing template）依赖应用程序。

### 错误修复

* Core：修复 wokflow hashing for MySQL.
* Core：让 `deepCopy` backward compatible.
* 编辑器：确保 `displayOptions` received the value from the resource locator component.
* 编辑器：禁用 settings link in executions view for unsaved workflows.
* 编辑器：确保 forms reliably save.
* 编辑器：修复interim updates in executions view 相关的问题。
* 编辑器：修复 for node creator search.
* 编辑器：限制 columns in table view to prevent the UI becoming unresponsive in the node details view.

## n8n@0.200.1

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.200.0...n8n@0.200.1)。
**发布日期：** 2022-10-28

这是一个错误修复（bug fix）版本。

### 错误修复

* API: do not reset the auth cookie on every request to GET `/login`.
* AWS SNS Trigger 节点：新增 missing jsonParse import.
* Core: avoid callstack with circular dependencies.
* 编辑器：解决the executions list auto-refresh, and with saving new workflows 相关的问题。
* Editor: redirect the outdated `/workflow` path.
* 编辑器：移除 a filter that prevented display of running executions.

## n8n@0.200.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.199.0...n8n@0.200.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-10-27

本版本包含编辑器改进、节点增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

* Core, editor: introduce workflow caller policy.
* Core: block workflow update on interim change.
* 编辑器：新增 a read-only state for nodes.
* 编辑器：新增 execution previews using the new **Executions** tab in the node view.
* 编辑器：改进 ments to node panel search.

### 节点增强

* Airtable Trigger 节点：新增 resource locator component.
* HTTP Request 节点：新增 options for raw JSON headers and queries.
* InvoiceNinja 节点：新增对 V5 的支持。
* Write Binary File 节点：新增 option to append to a file.

### 错误修复

* API: validate executions and workflow filter parameters.
* Core: amend typing for `jsonParse()` options.
* Core：修复 `predefinedCredentialType` in node graph item.
* Core：修复 canvas node execution skipping parent nodes.
* Core：修复 single node execution failing in `main` mode.
* Core：设置 JWT authentication token `sameSite` policy to `lax`.
* Core：更新 to imports in helpers.
* Editor: curb item method linting in single-item mode.
* 编辑器：停止 rendering expressions as HTML.
* Email Trigger node: backport V2 mark-seen-after processing to V1.
* Email Trigger 节点：改进 connection handling and credentials.
* HTTP Request 节点：修复 sending previously selected credentials.
* TheHive node: small fixes.

### 贡献者

[Bram Kn](https://github.com/bramkn)\
[Nicholas Penree](https://github.com/drudge)

## n8n@0.199.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.198.2...n8n@0.199.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-10-21

本版本包含新节点、改进的工作流 UI、性能改进和错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### New workflow experience

本版本带来一系列 UI 变更，旨在改善用户的工作流体验，包括：

* Removing the Start node, and adding help to guide users to find a trigger node.
* Improved node search.
* New nodes: Manual Trigger and Execute Workflow Trigger.
{% endhint %}

* Core: block workflow updates on interim changes.
* Core：启用 sending client credentials in the body of API calls.
* 编辑器：新增 automatic credential selection for new nodes.

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Compare node

Compare Datasets（比较数据集）节点帮助你比较两个输入流的数据。新节点的文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.comparedatasets)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Execute Workflow Trigger node

Execute Workflow Trigger（执行工作流触发器）在其他工作流发出请求时启动一个工作流。新节点的文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.executeworkflowtrigger)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Manual Trigger node

Manual Trigger（手动触发器）允许你通过点击 **Execute Workflow（执行工作流）** 启动工作流，没有自动运行的选项。新节点的文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.manualworkflowtrigger)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Schedule Trigger node

本版本引入 Schedule Trigger（计划触发器）节点，取代 Cron 节点。新节点的文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.scheduletrigger)找到。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* HubSpot node: you can now use your HubSpot credentials in the HTTP Request node to make a [custom API call](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/custom-api-actions-for-existing-nodes).
* Rundeck node: you can now use your Rundeck credentials in the HTTP Request node to make a [custom API call](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/custom-api-actions-for-existing-nodes).

### 错误修复

* 编辑器：修复 a hover bug in the bottom menu.
* 编辑器：解决 performance issues when opening a node, or editing a code node, with a large amount of data.
* 编辑器：确保 workflows always stop when clicking the stop button.
* 编辑器：修复 a bug that was causing text highlighting when mapping data in Firefox.
* 编辑器：确保 correct linting in the Code node editor.
* 编辑器：处理 null values in table view.
* Elasticsearch 节点：修复 a pagination issue.
* Google Drive 节点：修复 typo.
* HTTP Request node: avoid errors when a response doesn't provide a content type.
* n8n 节点：修复 a bug that was preventing the resource locator component from returning all items.

### 贡献者

[AndLLA](https://github.com/AndLLA)\
[Nicholas Penree](https://github.com/drudge)\
[vcrwr](https://github.com/vcrwr)

## n8n@0.198.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.197.1...n8n@0.198.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-10-14

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

请注意，此版本包含对 Merge（合并）节点的破坏性变更。你可以[在此](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#01980)阅读更多相关信息。
{% endhint %}

### 新功能

* 编辑器：更新 expressions display.
* 编辑器：更新 n8n-menu component.

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Code node

本版本引入 Code（代码）节点。该节点取代 Function 和 Function Item 节点。更多信息请参阅 [Code 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.code)文档。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Venafi TLS Protect Cloud Trigger node

在 Venafi Cloud 服务发生事件时启动工作流。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* Citrix ADC 节点：新增 Certificate Install operation.
* Kafka 节点：新增 a **Use key** option for messages.
* MySQL 节点：使用 resource locator component for table parameters, making it easier for users to browse and select their database fields from within n8n.

### 错误修复

* Core, Editor: prevent overlap between running and pinning data.
* Core: expression evaluation of processes now respects `N8N_BLOCK_ENV_ACCESS_IN_NODE`.
* 编辑器：确保 Axios base URL still works when hosted in a subfolder.
* 编辑器：修复 for horizontal scrollbar rendering.
* 编辑器：确保 menu closes promptly when loading a credentials page.
* Editor: menu UI fixes.
* Box 节点：修复 an issue that was causing the Create Folder operation to show extra items.
* GSuite Admin 节点：解决 issue that was causing the User Update operation to fail.
* GitLab Trigger 节点：确保 this node activates reliably.
* HTTP Request 节点：确保 OAuth credentials work properly with predefined credentials.
* KoboToolbox 节点：修复 hook logs.
* SeaTable 节点：确保 link items show in response.
* Zoom 节点：解决 an issue that was causing missing output items.

### 贡献者

[Jakob Backlund](https://github.com/jbacklund)\
[Yan Jouanique](https://github.com/Yann-J)

## n8n@0.197.1

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.197.0...n8n@0.197.1)。

## n8n@0.197.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.196.0...n8n@0.197.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-10-10

本版本包含六个围绕基础设施管理的新节点，同时在 JSON 输入视图中新增拖放数据映射支持，并包含错误修复。

### 新功能

* Core：改进 light versioning support in declarative node design.
* Editor UI: data mapping for JSON view. You can now map data using drag and drop from JSON view, as well as table view.

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### AWS Certificate Manager

新增与 AWS Certificate Manager 的集成。文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.awscertificatemanager)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### AWS Elastic Load Balancing

使用新的 AWS Elastic Load Balancing 节点，从你的工作流管理 AWS 负载均衡器。文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.awselb)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Citrix ADC

Citrix ADC 是面向单体应用和微服务应用的应用程序交付与负载均衡解决方案。文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.netscaleradc)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Cloudflare

Cloudflare 提供一系列服务来管理和保护你的网站。这个新节点允许你从工作流中管理 Cloudflare 的区域证书（zone certificates）。文档可以[在此](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.cloudflare)找到。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Venafi nodes

本版本包含两个新 Venafi 节点，用于与他们的 Protect TLS 服务集成。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

Crypto（加密）节点：新增 SHA3 支持。

### 错误修复

* CLI: cache generated assets in a user-writeable directory.
* Core：防止 excess runs when data is pinned in a trigger node.
* Core：确保 hook URLs always added correctly.
* Editor: a fix for an issue affecting linked items in combination with data pinning.
* 编辑器：解决 a bug with the binary data view.
* GitHub Trigger 节点：确保 trigger executes reliably.
* Microsoft Excel 节点：修复分页问题。
* Microsoft ToDo 节点：修复分页问题。

### 贡献者

[Stratos Theodorou](https://github.com/eeVoskos)

## n8n@0.196.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.195.5...n8n@0.196.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-09-30

本版本包含主要新功能：

* Better item linking
* New built-in variables and methods
* A redesigned main navigation
* New nodes, as well as an overhaul of the HTTP Request node

同时包含错误修复和节点增强。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Improved item linking

引入对条目关联（paired items）的改进支持。条目关联是 n8n 数据流中的一个关键概念。更多信息请参阅[数据条目关联](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/work-with-data/reference-data/link-data-items)。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Overhauled built-in variables

n8n 的[内置方法和变量](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/code-in-n8n)已全面改造，引入新变量，并在行为和命名上提供更大的一致性。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Redesigned main navigation

我们重新设计了主导航（左侧菜单），以创造更简单的用户体验。
{% endhint %}

#### Other new features

* Improved error text when loading options in a node.
* On reset, share unshared credentials with the instance owner.

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### n8n node

[n8n 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.n8n)允许你在工作流中使用 n8n API。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### WhatsApp Business Platform node

[WhatsApp Business Platform](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.whatsapp) 节点允许你在工作流中使用 WhatsApp Business Platform Cloud API。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* HTTP Request node: a major overhaul. It's now much simpler to build a custom API request. Refer to the [HTTP Request node documentation](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.httprequest) for more information.
* RabbitMQ Trigger node: now automatically reconnects on disconnect.
* Slack 节点：新增 'get many' operation for users.

### 错误修复

* Build: add typing for SSE channel.
* Build: fix lint issue.
* CLI：新增 git to all Docker images
* CLI：禁用 X-Powered-By: Express header.
* CLI：禁用 CORS on SSE connections in production.
* Core：移除 commented out lines.
* Core：删除 unused dependencies.
* Core：修复 and harmonize documentation links for nodes.
* Core：移除 --forceExit flag from CLI tests.
* 编辑器：新增 missing event handler to accordion component.
* 编辑器：修复 Storybook setup.
* 编辑器：确保 BASE\_URL replacement works correctly on Windows.
* 编辑器：修复 parameter input field focus.
* 编辑器：让 lodash aliases work on case-sensitive file systems.
* 编辑器：修复 an issue affecting copy-pasting workflows into pinned data in the code editor.
* 编辑器：确保 run data pagination selector displays when appropriate.
* 编辑器：确保 run selector can open.
* Editor: tidy up leftover i18n references in the node view.
* Editor: correct an i18n string.
* 编辑器：解决 slow loading times for node types, node creators, and push connections in the settings view.
* 节点：更新 descriptions in the Merge node
* 节点：确保 card ID property displays for completed checklists in the Trello node.
* 节点：修复 authentication for the new verions of WeKan.
* 节点：确保 form names list correctly in the Wufoo Trigger node.

### 贡献者

[Cristobal Schlaubitz Garcia](https://github.com/CxGarcia)

## n8n@0.195.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.194.0...n8n@0.195.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-09-21

本版本为 n8n 的 Cloud 平台引入用户管理和凭证共享，同时包含其他增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### User management and credential sharing for Cloud

本版本把 n8n 现有的[用户管理](https://app.gitbook.com/s/wMJrGrimpx3PxCJpUswm/manage-users-and-access)功能带到 Cloud，并引入一个新功能：[凭证共享](https://app.gitbook.com/s/wMJrGrimpx3PxCJpUswm/manage-credentials/share-credentials-securely)。凭证共享目前仅适用于 Cloud。
{% endhint %}

本版本还包括：

* 新增`resourceLocator` parameter type for nodes, and started upgrading n8n's built-in nodes to use it. This new option helps users who need to specify the ID of a record or item in an external service. For example, when using the Trello node, you can now search for a specific card by ID, URL, or do a free text search for card titles. Node builders can learn more about working with this new UI element in n8n's [UI elements](https://app.gitbook.com/s/r7wKI4I1BgdBCuq5Cvcx/create-nodes/build-your-node/reference/node-ui-elements) documentation
* Cache npm dependencies to improve performance on self-hosted n8n

### 错误修复

* Box 节点：修复 an issue that sometimes prevented response data from being returned.
* CLI：防止 n8n from crashing when it encounters an error in poll method.
* Core：防止 calls to constructor, to forbid arbitrary code execution.
* 编辑器：修复 output panel for Wait node executions.
* HTTP 节点：确保 instance doesn't crash when batching enabled.
* Public API: corrections to the OAuth schema.
* Xero 节点：修复 an issue that was causing line amount types to be ignored when creating new invoices.

### 贡献者

[Ikko Ashimine](https://github.com/eltociear)

## n8n@0.194.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.193.5...n8n@0.194.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-09-15

本版本包含新节点：Gmail 触发器、Google Cloud Storage 和 Adalo，同时包含对 Gmail 和 Merge 节点的重大改造。

### 新功能

* CLI：加载 all nodes and credentials code in isolation.
* Core, Editor UI: introduce support for node deprecation.
* Editor: implement HTML sanitization for Notification and Message components.

* CLI：确保 password reset triggers internal and external hooks.

## n8n@0.187.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.186.1...n8n@0.187.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-07-20

本版本包含几个主要新功能，包括：

* community nodes repository: a new way to build and share nodes
* Data pinning and data mapping: accelerate workflow development with better data manipulation functionality.

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Community nodes repository

本版本引入[社区节点仓库](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/community-nodes/installation-and-management)。这允许开发者以 npm 包的形式构建和共享节点。用户可以直接在 n8n 中安装社区构建的节点。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Data pinning

[数据固定（Data pinning）](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/work-with-data/pin-and-mock-data)允许你在工作流开发期间冻结和编辑数据。数据固定意味着保存节点的输出数据，并在未来的工作流执行中使用已保存的数据，而不是获取新数据。这避免了开发工作流时重复的 API 调用，减少对外部系统的调用，并加快工作流开发。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Data mapping

本版本为[数据映射](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/work-with-data/reference-data)引入拖放界面，作为无需使用表达式即可映射数据的快捷方式。
{% endhint %}

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Simplify authentication setup for node creators

本版本引入一种更简单的节点构建授权处理方式。所有凭证现在都应包含一个 `authenticate` 属性，规定凭证如何在请求中使用。n8n 还简化了认证类型：不再需要指定认证类型并使用正确的接口，你现在可以把类型设置为 `"generic"`，并使用 `IAuthenticateGeneric` 接口。

你可以把这种方法用于任何在请求头、请求体或查询字符串中发送数据的认证方法，包括 bearer 和 basic auth 等方法。对于需要多次调用或不需要传递认证数据的更复杂认证类型（包括 OAuth），你不能使用这种方法。

关于新认证语法的示例，请参阅 n8n 的 [Asana 节点](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/nodes-base/credentials/AsanaApi.credentials.ts)。

```js
// in AsanaApi.credentials.ts
import {
	IAuthenticateGeneric,
	ICredentialType,
	INodeProperties,
} from 'n8n-workflow';

export class AsanaApi implements ICredentialType {
	name = 'asanaApi';
	displayName = 'Asana API';
	documentationUrl = 'asana';
	properties: INodeProperties[] = [
		{
			displayName: 'Access Token',
			name: 'accessToken',
			type: 'string',
			default: '',
		},
	];

	authenticate: IAuthenticateGeneric = {
		type: 'generic',
		properties: {
			headers: {
				Authorization: '=Bearer {{$credentials.accessToken}}',
			},
		},
	};
}
```
{% endhint %}

#### Other new features

* 新增`preAuthentication` method to credentials
* 新增more credentials tests
* 引入automatic fixing for paired item information in some scenarios

### 节点增强

* [ERPNext node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.erpnext): add credential tests, and add support for unauthorized certs.
* [Google Drive node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googledrive): add support for move to trash.
* [Mindee node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mindee): support new version.
* [Notion node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.notion): support ignoring the Notion URL property if empty.
* [Shopify node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.shopify): add OAuth support.

### 错误修复

* API：新增 missing node settings parameters.
* API: validate static data value for resource workflow.
* Baserow 节点：修复 an issue preventing table names from loading.
* 编辑器：隐藏 **Execute previous node** button when in read-only mode.
* 编辑器：隐藏 tabs if there's only one branch.
* Roundup of link fixes in nodes.

### 贡献者

[Florian Bachmann](https://github.com/baflo) [Olivier Aygalenq](https://github.com/oaygalenq)

本版本包含错误修复和节点增强。

### 新功能

* 更新multiple credentials with tests, and add support for custom operations

### 节点增强

* [AWS DynamoDB node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.awsdynamodb): improve error handling and add an optional GetAll Scan FilterExpression.
* [Customer.io node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.customerio): add support for tracking region selection.
* [Elasticsearch node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.elasticsearch): add 'Source Excludes' and 'Source Includes' options to the Document: getAll operation. Add credential tests, index pipelines, and index refresh.
* [Freshworks CRM node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.freshworkscrm): add search and lookup functionality.
* [JIRA node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.jiratrigger): add optional query authentication.
* [Postgres node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.postgres): improve handling of large numbers.
* [Redis node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.redis): add push and pop operations.
* [Rename node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.renamekeys): add regex replace.
* [Spreadsheet file node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.converttofile): allow skipping headers when writing spreadsheets.

### 错误修复

* 编辑器：修复 an error that occurred after repeated executions.
* [EmailReadImap node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.emailimap): improve handling of network problems.
* [Google Drive node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googledrive): process input items using the list operation.
* [Telegram node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.telegram): fix for a bug affecting sending binary data (images, documents and so on).

## n8n@0.185.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.184.0...n8n@0.185.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-07-05

本版本新增一个节点 Google Ads，同时包含错误修复、节点增强，以及一个小的核心新增。

### 新功能

Core：把 `action` 参数添加到 INodePropertyOptions。该参数现在在构建节点时可用。

### 新节点

[Google Ads 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googleads)：n8n 现在提供 Google Ads 节点，允许你从 Google 广告系列获取数据。

### 节点增强

* [DeepL node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.deepl): Add support for longer text fields, and add credentials tests.
* [Facebook Graph API node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.facebookgraphapi): Add support for Facebook Graph API 14.
* [JIRA node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.jira): Add support for the simplified option with rendered fields.
* [Webflow Trigger node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.webflowtrigger): Reduce the chance of webhook duplication. Add a credentials test.
* [WordPress node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.wordpress): Add a post template option.

### 错误修复

* [HubSpot node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspot): Fix for search endpoints.
* [KoboToolbox node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.kobotoolbox): Improve attachment matching logic and GeoJSON Polygon format.
* [Odoo node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.odoo): Prevent possible issues with some custom fields.
* Sticky note 节点：修复以下问题 was causing the main header to hide.
* [Todoist node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.todoist): Improve multi-item support.

### 贡献者

[cgobrech](https://github.com/cgobrech) [pemontto](https://github.com/pemontto) [Yann Jouanique](https://github.com/Yann-J) [Zapfmeister](https://github.com/Zapfmeister)

## n8n@0.184.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.183.0...n8n@0.184.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-06-29

本版本包括：

* New core features
* Enhancements to the Clockify node.
* Bug fixes.

### 新功能

* You can now access `getBinaryDataBuffer` in the pre-send method.
* n8n now exposes the item index being processed by a node.
* Migrated the expressions templating engine to [n8n's fork of riot-tmpl](https://github.com/n8n-io/tmpl).

### 节点增强

[Clockify 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.clockify)：新增三个资源：Client、User 和 Workspace，并新增对自定义 API 调用的支持。

### 错误修复

* Core：修复 an error with logging circular links in JSON.
* Editor UI: now display the full text of long error messages.
* Editor UI: fix for an issue with credentials rendering when the node has no parameters.
* [Cortex node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.cortex): fix an issue preventing all analyzers being returned.
* [HTTP Request node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.httprequest): ensure all OAuth2 credentials work with this node.
* [LinkedIn node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.linkedin): fix an issue with image preview.
* [Salesforce node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.salesforce): fix an issue that was causing the lead status to not use the new name when name is updated.
* 修复required/optional parameters

### 贡献者

[pemontto](https://github.com/pemontto)

## n8n@0.183.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.182.1...n8n@0.183.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-06-21

本版本包含节点增强、错误修复，以及改进的触发器节点面板。

### 新功能

**Trigger（触发器）** 输入面板的增强：使用触发器节点时，你现在会看到一个 **INPUT** 视图，指导如何把数据加载到触发器中。

### 节点增强

* [HubSpot node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspot): you can now assign a stage on ticket update.
* [Todoist node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.todoist): it's now possible to move tasks between sections.
* [Twake node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.twake): updated icon, credential test added, and added support for custom operations.

### 错误修复

* Core：不要 allow OPTIONS requests from any source.
* Core: GET `/workflows/:id` now returns tags.
* Core：确保 predefined credentials show up in the HTTP Request node.
* Core：返回 correct error message on Axios error.
* Core：更新 to the expressions allow-list and deny-list.

### 贡献者

[Bryce Sheehan](https://github.com/ctrl-freak) [Rahimli Rahim](https://github.com/rahimlis)

本版本引入公共 API。

### New feature highlights

{% hint style="info" %}
#### The n8n public API

本版本引入 n8n 公共 REST API。使用 n8n 的公共 API，你可以用编程方式执行许多与在 GUI 中相同的任务。该 API 包含内置的 Swagger UI 游乐场。更多信息请参阅 [API 文档](https://app.gitbook.com/s/r7wKI4I1BgdBCuq5Cvcx/n8n-api)。
{% endhint %}

### Other new features

* **Core**: you can now block user access to environment variables using the `N8N_BLOCK_ENV_ACCESS_IN_NODE` variable.

### 错误修复

* **Core**: properly resolve expressions in declarative style nodes.

## n8n@0.180.0

本版本推出 PostBin 新节点，以及各种节点增强和错误修复。

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### PostBin node

PostBin 充当标准 HTTP 库的包装器，可用于通过发送请求测试任意 API/Webhook，并提供更高级的响应分析方式。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* [RabbitMQ Trigger node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.rabbitmqtrigger): Made message acknowledgement and parallel processing configurable.
* [ServiceNow node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.servicenow): Added support for attachments.
* [Todoist node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.todoist): Added support for specifying the parent task when adding and listing tasks.

### 错误修复

* Core：修复 migrations on non-public Postgres schema.
* **Core**: Mitigated possible XSS vulnerability when importing workflow templates.
* **Editor UI**: fixed erroneous hover state detection close to the sticky note button.
* **Editor UI**: fixed display behavior of credentials assigned to versioned nodes.
* [Discord node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.discord): Fixed rate limit handling.
* [Gmail node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.gmail): Fixed sending attachments in filesystem data mode.
* [Google Sheets node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googlesheets): Fixed an error preventing the _Use Header Names as JSON Paths_ option from working as expected.
* [Nextcloud node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.nextcloud): Updated the node so the list:folder operation works with Nextcloud version 24.
* [YouTube node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.youtube): Fixed problem with uploading large files.

## n8n@0.178.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.177.0...n8n@0.178.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-05-24

本版本新增在 HTTP Request 节点中复用现有凭证的支持，让对 n8n 已有集成的 API 执行自定义操作更容易。

此版本还包括节点视图的改进，提供更详细的传入数据信息，以及一些错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Credential reuse for custom API operations

n8n 提供数百个节点，让你可以创建连接多个产品的工作流。然而，有些节点并未包含产品 API 支持的所有操作。你可以通过使用 HTTP Request 节点发起自定义 API 调用来绕开这一限制。

设置 API 调用最复杂的部分之一是管理认证。为简化这一点，n8n 现在提供一种方式，可以在 HTTP Request 节点中使用现有的凭证类型（与 n8n 节点关联的凭证）。

更多信息请参阅[自定义 API 操作](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/custom-api-actions-for-existing-nodes)。

#### Node details view

改进的节点视图，显示更多关于节点输入的细节。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

[Salesforce 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.salesforce)：新增 **Country（国家/地区）** 字段。

### 错误修复

* **Editor UI**: don't display the dividing line unless necessary.
* **Editor UI**: don't display the 'Welcome' sticky in template workflows.
* [Slack Node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.slack): Fix the kick operation for the channel resource.

## n8n@0.175.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.174.0...n8n@0.175.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-05-02

本版本新增节点版本化支持，并包含节点增强和错误修复。

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Node versioning

0.175.0 新增对轻量级节点版本化方法的支持。一个节点可以包含多个版本，允许小步版本递增而无需重复代码。要使用此功能，请把节点中的 `version` 参数改为数组，并添加你的版本号（包括现有版本）。然后你可以在 `displayOptions` 中用 `@version` 访问版本参数（以控制 n8n 显示哪个版本）。你也可以在 `execute` 函数中用 `const nodeVersion = this.getNode().typeVersion;` 查询版本。
{% endhint %}

### 节点增强

* [Google Sheets node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googlesheets): n8n now handles header names formatted as JSON paths.
* [Microsoft Dynamics CRM node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.microsoftdynamicscrm): add support for regions other than North America.
* [Telegram node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.telegram): add support for querying chat administrators.

### 错误修复

* **core**: fixed an issue that was causing n8n to apply authentication checks, even when user management was disabled.
* **core**: n8n now skips credentials checks for disabled nodes.
* **editor**: fix a bug affecting touchscreen monitors.
* [HubSpot node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspot): fix for search operators.
* [SendGrid node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.sendgrid): fixed an issue with sending attachments.
* [Wise node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.wise): respect the time parameter on `get: exchangeRate`.

### 贡献者

[Jack Rudenko](https://github.com/erudenko) [MC Naveen](https://github.com/mcnaveen) [vcrwr](https://github.com/vcrwr)

## n8n@0.174.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.173.1...n8n@0.174.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-04-25

### 新功能

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Sticky Notes

本版本新增便签（Sticky Notes）功能，允许你在工作流上做注释和评论。更多信息请参阅[便签](https://app.gitbook.com/s/rPN1zU5jaYNvwH7RzxqA/understand-workflows/workflow-components/add-notes-and-documentation)。
{% endhint %}

### Enhancements

* **core**: allow external OAuth connection. This enhancement adds support for connecting OAuth apps without access to n8n.
* All AWS nodes now support AWS temporary credentials.
* [Google Sheets node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googlesheets): Added upsert support.
* [Microsoft Teams node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.microsoftteams): adds several enhancements:
  * An option to limit groups to "member of", rather than retrieving the whole directory.
  * An option to get all tasks from a plan instead of just a group member.
  * Autocompletion for plans, buckets, labels, and members in update fields for tasks.
* [MongoDB node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mongodb): you can now parse dates using dot notation.

### 错误修复

* [Calendly Trigger node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.calendlytrigger): updated the logo.
* [Microsoft OneDrive node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.microsoftonedrive): fixed an issue that was preventing upload of files with special characters in the file name.
* [QuickBooks node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.quickbooks): fixed a pagination issue.

### 贡献者

[Basit Ali](https://github.com/BasitAli) [Cody Stamps](https://github.com/crstamps2) [Luiz Eduardo de Oliveira](https://github.com/luizeof) [Oliver Trajceski](https://github.com/SchnapsterDog) [pemontto](https://github.com/pemontto) [Ryan Goggin](https://github.com/Goggin)

## n8n@0.173.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.172.0...n8n@0.173.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-04-19

### 新节点

{% hint style="info" %}
#### Markdown node

[Markdown 节点](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.markdown)：新增 Markdown 节点，用于在 Markdown 和 HTML 之间转换。
{% endhint %}

### Enhancements

**编辑器**：你现在可以把节点从节点面板拖放到画布上。

### 节点增强

* [Discord node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.discord): additional fields now available when sending a message to Discord.
* [GoogleBigQuery](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googlebigquery): added support for service account authentication.
* [Google Cloud Realtime Database node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googlecloudrealtimedatabase): you can now select a region.
* [PagerDuty node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.pagerduty): now supports more detail in incidents.
* [Slack node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.slack): added support for blocks in Slack message update.

### 错误修复

* **core**: make the email for user management case insensitive.
* **core**: add `rawBody` for XML requests.
* **editor**: fix a glitch that caused dropdowns to break after adding expressions.
* **editor**: reset text input value when closed with `Esc`.
* [Discourse node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.discourse): fix an issue that was causing incomplete results when getting posts. Added a credentials test.
* [Zendesk Trigger node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.zendesktrigger): remove deprecated targets, replace with webhooks.
* [Zoho node](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.zohocrm): fix pagination issue.

### 贡献者

[Florian Metz](https://github.com/Timeraa) [Francesco Pongiluppi](https://github.com/willywongi) [Mark Steve Samson](https://github.com/marksteve) [Mike Quinlan](https://github.com/mjquinlan2000)

## n8n@0.171.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.170.0...n8n@0.171.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-04-03

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

请注意，此版本包含破坏性变更。你可以[在此](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#01710)阅读更多相关信息。
{% endhint %}

本版本专注于错误修复和节点增强，另有一个新功能，以及对 GraphQL 节点的一个破坏性变更。

### Breaking change to GraphQL node

GraphQL 节点现在在响应包含错误时会报错。如果你使用此节点，可以选择：

* Do nothing: a GraphQL response containing an error will now cause the workflow to fail.
* 更新your GraphQL node settings: set **Continue on Fail** to true to allow the workflow to continue even when the GraphQL response contains an error

### 新功能

你现在可以从工作流中的单个节点下载二进制数据。

### Enhanced nodes

* [Emelia Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.emelia) Add Campaign > Duplicate functionality.
* [FTP Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.ftp) Add option to recursively create directories on rename.
* [Mautic Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mautic) Add credential test and allow trailing slash in host.
* [Microsoft Teams Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.microsoftteams) Add chat message support.
* [Mocean Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mocean) Add 'Delivery Report URL' option and credential tests.
* [ServiceNow Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.servicenow) Add basicAuth support and fix getColumns loadOptions.
* [Strava Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.strava) Add 'Get Streams' operation.

### 错误修复

* **core:** Fix crash on webhook when last node did not return data
* [EmailReadImap Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.emailimap) Fix issue that crashed process if node was configured wrong.
* [Google Tasks Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.googletasks) Fix 'Show Completed' option and hide title field where not needed.
* [NocoDB Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.nocodb) Fix pagination.
* [Salesforce Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.salesforce) Fix issue that 'status' did not get used for Case => Create & Update

## n8n@0.169.0

查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.168.2...n8n@0.169.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-03-20

本版本包括：

* New functionality for existing nodes
* A new node for Linear
* Bug fixes
* And a license change!

### New license

本版本更改了 n8n 的许可证，从 [Apache 2.0 with Commons Clause](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/181ba3c4e236279b65d102a8a33ae6896f160487/LICENSE.md) 改为[可持续使用许可证（Sustainable Use License）](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/LICENSE.md)。

此变更旨在澄清 n8n 的许可证条款，以及 n8n 作为 fair-code（公平代码）项目的定位。

更多关于新许可证的信息，请阅读[许可证](https://app.gitbook.com/s/ukPPOMQ6NId4gpAIkPXa/sustainable-use-license)。

### 新节点

* [Linear Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.linear) Add Linear Node.

### Enhanced nodes

* [HTTP Request Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.httprequest) Allow Delete requests with body.
* [KoBoToolbox Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.kobotoolbox) Add KoBoToolbox Regular and Trigger Node.
* [Mailjet Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mailjet) Add credential tests and support for sandbox, JSON parameters & variables.
* [Mattermost Node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mattermost) Add support for Channel search.

### Other improvements

* 新增support for reading IDs from file with executeBatch command

### 错误修复

* [GitHub node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.github) Fix credential tests and File List operation.
* [Telegram node:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.telegram) Fix sending binary data when disable notification is set.

### 贡献者

* [Manuel](https://github.com/tennox)
* [Marcin Kozey](https://github.com/marcinkoziej)
* [Matthew Walther](https://github.com/mashiox)
* [Yann Jouanique](https://github.com/Yann-J)

## n8n@0.168.0

完整变更列表请查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n%400.167.0...n8n@0.168.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-03-14

### New feature: user management

n8n 中的用户管理允许你邀请人员到你的自托管 n8n 实例中协作，包括：

* Login and password management
* Adding and removing users
* Two account types: owner and member

更多信息请查看[用户管理文档](https://app.gitbook.com/s/wMJrGrimpx3PxCJpUswm/manage-users-and-access)。

## n8n@0.167.0

完整变更列表请查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n%400.166.0...n8n@0.167.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-03-13

### Highlights

#### Luxon and JMESPath

0.167.0 新增对两个新库的支持：

* [Luxon](https://moment.github.io/luxon/#/): a JavaScript library for working with date and time
* [JMESPath](https://jmespath.org/): a query language for JSON

你可以在代码编辑器和表达式中使用 Luxon 和 JMESPath。

#### New expressions variables

我们新增了两个变量，以简化在表达式中处理日期和时间：

* `$now`: a Luxon object containing the current timestamp. Equivalent to DateTime.now().
* `$today`: a Luxon object containing the current timestamp, rounded down to the day. Equivalent to DateTime.now().set({ hour: 0, minute: 0, second: 0, millisecond: 0 }).

#### Negative operations in If and Switch nodes

让对字符串执行否定操作更容易。

本版本为数字新增一个操作：

* Is Not Empty

以及为字符串新增以下操作：

* Not Ends With
* Regex Not Match
* Not Starts With
* Is Not Empty

此外，Regex 现在改名为 Regex Match。

#### New node: Redis Trigger

新增 Redis Trigger（Redis 触发器）节点，你现在可以根据 Redis 事件启动工作流。

* [Redis Trigger:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.redistrigger) Added a Redis Trigger node.

### Core functionality

* 新增support for [Luxon](https://moment.github.io/luxon/#/) and [JMESPath](https://jmespath.org/)
* 新增two new expressions variables, `$now` and `$today`
* 新增more negative operations for numbers and strings
* 新增link to the course from the help menu

### Nodes

* [Facebook Graph API:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.facebookgraphapi) Added suport for Facebook Graph API 13.
* [HubSpot:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspot) Added suport for private app token authentication.
* [MongoDB:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mongodb) Added the aggregate operation.
* [Redis Trigger:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.redistrigger) Added a Redis Trigger node.
* [Redis:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.redis) Added support for publish operations.
* [Strapi:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.strapi) Added support for Strapi 4.
* [WordPress:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.wordpress) Added status as an option to getAll post requests.

### Bugfixes

* Google Calendar node now correctly applies timezones when creating, updating, and scheduling all day events
* Fixed a bug that occasionally caused n8n to crash, or shut down workflows unexpectedly.
* You can now use long credential type names with Postgres.

### 贡献者

* [Luiz Eduardo de Oliveira Fonseca](https://github.com/luizeof)
* [Vitaliy Fratkin](https://github.com/viiy)
* [sol](https://github.com/5pecia1)
* [vcrwr](https://github.com/vcrwr)
* [FFTDB](https://github.com/FFTDB)

* [Wise:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.wise) Fixed a bug causing API requests requiring Strong Customer Authentication (SCA) to fail.

## n8n@0.165.0

完整变更列表请查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n@0.164.1...n8n@0.165.0)。
**发布日期：** 2022-02-28

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

Please note that this version contains breaking changes. You can read more about them [here](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#01650).
{% endhint %}

### 新节点

* [Onfleet](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.onfleet)

### Enhanced nodes

* [Asana:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.asana) Added Create operation to the Project resource.
* [Mautic:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.mautic) Added Edit Contact Points, Edit Do Not Contact List, Send Email operations to Contact resource. Also added new Segment Email resource.
* [Notion (Beta):](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.notion) Added support for rollup fields to the Simplify Output option. Also added the Parent ID to the Get All operation of the Block resource.
* [Pipedrive:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.pipedrive) Added Marketing Status field to the Create operation of the Person resource, also added User ID field to the Create and Update operations of the Person resource.

### Core functionality

* 新增support for workflow templates
* Fixed a bug causing credentials tests to fail for versioned nodes.
* Fixed a build problem by addind dependencies `@types/lodash.set` to the `workflow` package and `@types/uuid` to the `core` package.
* Fixed an error causing some resources to ignore a non-standard `N8N_PATH` value.
* Fixed an error preventing the placeholder text from being shown when entering credentials.
* Improved error handling for telemetry-related errors.

### 错误修复

* Orbit: Fixed a bug causing API requests to use an incorrect workspace identifier.
* [TheHive:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.thehive) Fixed a bug causing the Ignore SSL Issues option to be applied incorrectly.

### 贡献者

[alexwitkowski](https://github.com/awitkowski0), [Iñaki Breinbauer](https://github.com/quansenB), [lsemaj](https://github.com/jamesliupenn), [Luiz Eduardo de Oliveira Fonseca](https://github.com/luizeof), [Rodrigo Correia](https://github.com/rodrigoscdc), [Santiago Botero Ruiz](https://github.com/yoky-devsavant), [Saurabh Kashyap](https://github.com/saurabharch), [Ugo Bataillard](https://github.com/knshiro)

* [HTTP Request:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.httprequest) Added support for query-based authentication.
* [Home Assistant:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.homeassistant) Added support for loading possible options in the Domain, Service, and Entity ID fields.
* [One Simple API:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.onesimpleapi) Added support for Social Profile resources.
* [PagerDuty:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.pagerduty) Write scope is now requested upon authentication against the PagerDuty OAuth2 API.

### Core Functionality

* 新增frontend for value surveys
* Fixed an issue preventing the recommendation logic from working as expected after selecting a work area
* 修复wrong exit code was sent when running n8n on an unsupported version of Node.js
* 修复node options would disappear on hovering when a node isn't selected
* 修复execution id was missing when running n8n in queue mode
* 修复execution data was missing when waiting for a webhook in queue mode
* Improved error handling when the n8n port is already in use
* Improved diagnostic events
* Removed toast notification on webhook deletion, added toast notification after node is copied
* Removed default trigger tooltip for polling trigger nodes

### 错误修复

* [APITemplate.io:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.apitemplateio) Fixed a bug where the Create operation on the Image resource would fail when the Download option isn't enabled.
* [HubSpot:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspot) Fixed authentication for new HubSpot applications by using granular scopes when authenticating against the HubSpot OAuth2 API.
* [HubSpot Trigger:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspottrigger) Fixed authentication for new HubSpot applications by using granular scopes when authenticating against the HubSpot Developer API.
* [Jira Software:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.jira) Fixed an issue where the Reporter field would not work as expected on Jira Server instances.
* [Salesforce:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.salesforce) Fixed a typo preventing the value in the amount field of from being saved.

### 贡献者

[pemontto](https://github.com/pemontto), [Jascha Lülsdorf](https://github.com/buelsenfrucht), [Jonathan Bennetts](https://github.com/Joffcom)

* [Salesforce:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.salesforce) Fixed a typo affecting the Type field of the Opportunity resource.

* [HubSpot:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/app-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.hubspot) Added dropdown selection for Properties and Properties with History filters for Get All Deals operations.

## n8n@0.135.0

完整变更列表请查看此版本的 [commits](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/compare/n8n%400.134.0...n8n@0.135.0)。
**发布日期：** 2021-08-22

{% hint style="warning" %}
**破坏性变更**

Please note that this version contains breaking changes. You can read more about it [here](https://github.com/n8n-io/n8n/blob/master/packages/cli/BREAKING-CHANGES.md#01350). The features that introduced the breaking changes have been flagged below.
{% endhint %}

### 新节点

* [Form.io 触发器](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.formiotrigger)
* [Formstack 触发器](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/trigger-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.formstacktrigger)
* [Wait](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.wait)

### Core Functionality

* In-node method for accessing binary data is now asynchronous and a helper function for this has been implemented.
* Credentials are now loaded from the database on-demand.
* Webhook UUIDs are automatically updated when duplicating a workflow.
* 修复referencing values before loops

### 错误修复

* [Interval:](https://app.gitbook.com/s/BKcbOzIWja8NfqKDcqHc/builtin/core-nodes/n8n-nodes-base.scheduletrigger) Fixed issue where entering too large a value (> 2147483647ms) resulted in an interval of 1sec being used rather than an error.
